  {"id": 109, "type_id": 1, "project_id": 3, "subject": "PXA-2023-0629 Corwyn Office Complex PV \u2014 PTO for 08/04/2025: HOLD (RTU supplied through the POI recloser; TCS-02 \u00a75)", "description": "**Determination (DER Program Office, 07/14/2025): HOLD.** Permission to Operate **cannot issue on 08/04/2025** on the record as it stands at 07/14/2025. The advice to Lena Kovalchuk (SOC Shift Supervisor) is in memo `/filesystem/Corwyn_PTO_Memo.docx`, for signature by the Office's reviewing engineer (P.E.). Hold unless the Office confirms in writing before 08/04/2025 that every item below is closed. There is no conditional or interim PTO: TCS-02 \u00a75 has no interim provision, and ISA 6.1 allows no energization before PTO except for the witness test.\n\n### What stands in the way: telemetry cannot be live at the moment PTO is issued (TCS-02 \u00a75; ISA 6.2, 7.2)\n- The RTU's station AC service is Corwyn's provision (TCS-02 \u00a73.1, \u00a73.4; ISA 5.2). It runs PP-1 circuit 1 (through the UPS in the RTU enclosure) \u2190 TX-1 \u2190 40 A breaker in SWBD-PV \u2190 T-1 \u2190 PSW-1 \u2190 POI recloser (E-101 Rev C), so it is live only while the recloser is closed.\n- ISA 6.1 keeps the recloser open at all times before PTO except for the witness test. Corwyn's ISA has no other provision for energizing the facility before PTO.\n- Distribution Operations opened the recloser at 15:35 on 07/08/2025 after the witness test (WT-0629-01). The UPS ran out at about 21:05 on Brantley's 5.5 h figure, and no later than about 21:35 on the Corvane table (20 W, 5.99 h). The RTU has been dark since. The SOC confirmation dated 07/08/2025 on CX-CW-01 Rev 2 was taken with the recloser closed, so it is not the \u00a75 confirmation.\n- The per-inverter points (link COM-RTU, E-401 Rev C) come straight from the inverters, which take control power from their own ac output terminals. They are also dead while the facility is de-energized.\n- The same arrangement prevents SCADA supervision of the recloser at the first parallel operation (ISA 6.3; DPS-07 \u00a76.3). After PTO it would come back with every long recloser opening (ISA 8.3, 8.4).\n\n### To be settled before PTO, and by whom\n**Corwyn Realty Partners (through Brantley Electric Company, permit holder)**\n1. Re-feed the RTU enclosure from a supply that stays energized with the POI recloser open, not from PP-1/TX-1/SWBD-PV. One option is a new dedicated branch circuit from the office complex's own service (LP-2 already feeds DAS-1). Keep the UPS. Issue revised E-101 and E-401.\n2. Show that the UPS gives at least 4 h at the actual enclosure load after the data sheet's 20 % derate, and that it stays within its \u221220 \u00b0C rating at the site's \u221224 \u00b0C design low. Nothing on the record shows the load behind the 5.5 h figure; by the data sheet's formula it implies about 25 W.\n3. Obtain City of Wrenford permit and inspection approval of the new circuit before it is energized.\n4. Make any customer-side change SCADA/EMS requires so the per-inverter points present as valid at the moment of issue (ISA 7.2).\n5. Keep the facility out of parallel until written PTO (ISA 6.1, 6.2).\n\n**City of Wrenford Department of Inspections**\n6. Permit and inspect the new circuit. The 07/02/2025 final approval under WEP-25-0317 does not cover new work.\n\n**SCADA/EMS Engineering (Marcus Deele)**\n7. Specify the station-service source in the site's RTU installation drawing (TCS-02 \u00a73.1) and state the enclosure load.\n8. Accept the new supply and confirm the RTU is back on line.\n9. State in writing how the per-inverter points will present as valid at the moment of issue (TCS-02 \u00a75(c), \u00a76.1).\n\n**DER Program Office \u2014 Contracts**\n10. Accept the change in writing: as a construction-package revision under ISA 5.2, and by change order under ISA 8.7 and 12.2 so far as it touches the Facility. Capacity and POI are unchanged, so DIS-01 \u00a77 and Tariff \u00a712.6 are not engaged.\n\n**DER Program Office \u2014 Commissioning (S. Rennick)**\n11. Tell Corwyn (Nadia Ferreira-Holt) now that 08/04/2025 cannot be confirmed, and why (ISA 13.3).\n12. Correct CX-CW-01 so the SOC-confirmation line reads \"at the PTO request\".\n13. Get DPE's written confirmation that the 06/18/2025 DER-side protection review (DPS-07 \u00a74.3) covers the Rev C drawings of 06/24/2025. This is a records point, not a hold item unless Rev C changed DER-side protection.\n14. Confirm each closure to the SOC in writing.\n\n**SOC Shift Supervisor on shift (Lena Kovalchuk)**\n15. Record the hold on the SOC PTO-Hold Log.\n16. When PTO is requested, confirm live telemetry at the moment of issue and record it against the file (TCS-02 \u00a75). Observe the first parallel operation with the recloser under SCADA supervision (ISA 6.3).\n\n### Can it be done before 08/04/2025?\nPhysically, yes: it is one 120 V branch circuit and a UPS check, with 15 business days available. But the steps run in sequence, none had started on the record at 07/14/2025, item 9 is open, and the timing depends on Corwyn's contractor and the City's inspections. **Plan on holding.** The earliest PTO is the first date after the last item closes, on the SOC's \u00a75 confirmation and subject to the separate reviews.\n\n### Rest of the record: in order\n- ISA executed 09/12/2024.\n- Witness test WT-0629-01 complete and passed (M. Halbrook, P.E.).\n- Certificate of conformance VX-CC-25-0522 on file; VX-140C on CEL-2025-Q2, row 18.\n- City of Wrenford final approval 07/02/2025.\n- Revenue meter RM-0629 set and tested 07/08/2025.\n- No payment notice, Notice of Impending Withdrawal or hold-list entry on the record.\n\n### Not covered (under separate review)\n- Installation, configuration and commissioning tests of the Company's RTU and POI recloser (SCADA/EMS; DPE).\n- POI recloser settings and the protection coordination study (DPE).\n- Queue position (Meera Vellanki).\n- The system impact study and its conditions (Ivo Grantham, DER Planning Group).\n\nThe hold stands whatever these conclude.", "due_date": "2025-08-04", "category_id": null, "status_id": 7, "assigned_to_id": 31, "priority_id": 9, "version_id": null, "author_id": 4, "done_ratio": null, "estimated_hours": null, "start_date": "2025-07-14", "parent_id": null, "responsible_id": null, "budget_id": null, "story_points": null, "remaining_hours": null, "derived_estimated_hours": null, "schedule_manually": "true", "duration": 16, "ignore_non_working_days": "false", "derived_remaining_hours": null, "derived_done_ratio": null}
